# tfc.dll <-> tfc.so vtable cross-map

Per class: the DLL (MSVC) vtable slot index, the method name (from tfc.so), and the DLL function address. Hook a virtual by its **DLL slot** index.

The DLL has one destructor slot where tfc.so has two (complete+deleting); every slot after the destructor is therefore tfc.so_slot - 1.
